## I208 - Blender ship model refresh (cgs-dev-story) (ST-208)
- Summary: ST-208 closed. The 254-tri I103 ship GLB (cone hull + 224-tri UV-sphere
  cockpit) is replaced by a hand-built 40-tri faceted delta: one merged hull mesh
  (hull + swept knife-edge wings + dorsal fin, 2 materials) + one recessed 6-tri
  canopy gem, per the I207 video grounding (Imphenzia spaceship series: angular
  delta, recessed faceted canopy, 2-material accent).
- Story Goal: ship reads as an angular low-poly delta with a glowing recessed gem
  in combat (not a ball), <=300 tris, 3 draw calls, wingspan 2.5 / length 1.9,
  watertight, loads via the existing GLB pipeline with no code change.
- Owner: /root (Blender MCP 5.2.3 + repo). Dependencies: ST-207 (GLB pipeline +
  assetGlbs.test.js) - met at I207.
- Acceptance: GLB loads and swaps (ship_body removed), nodes VR_Ship_Hull +
  VR_Ship_Cockpit, all 3 material names present, tris <= 300, wingspan 2.5 +/-10%,
  length 1.9 +/-10%, tail end z = -0.95, watertight hull, browser smoke console
  0/0, silhouette visually confirmed.
- Build notes: scene rebuilt cleanly from the verified face list (the I207-era
  partial run-3 mesh was unreliable). Watertightness proven with the correct
  bmesh check (len(e.link_faces) != 2 = boundary edge; the handoff pair-counter
  over bm.edges is always 1 by construction and is NOT a valid manifold check).
  Hull = 14 faces (13 I207-listed + rear-bottom triangle (L_T,mT,R_T) the partial
  build lacked; Euler 13-25+14=2) + 6 fin quads = closed 2-shell mesh; canopy = 6
  tris, open base ring only (embedded below the ridge, by design). Exporter axis
  quirk found + compensated: this Blender glTF exporter maps Blender (x,y,z) to
  GLB (x, z, -y) - the nose was landing on GLB +Y (up). Meshes pre-rotated
  (x,y,z) -> (x,-z,y) so the GLB is nose +Z, up +Y per the render contract
  (GLB bounds verified: x +/-1.25, y -0.08..0.30, z -0.95..0.95). Blender material

## I209 - Blender asteroid set refresh (cgs-dev-story) (ST-209)
- Summary: ST-209 closed. The three I103-era rock GLBs (plain jittered
  icospheres) are replaced by a rebuilt set with stronger displacement and
  baked vertex-color shading: per-size seeded 3-octave directional caggy
  field + white-noise facet break, face-cluster dents (L 10, M 4, S 6),
  four real inset craters on M (+12 tris), per-vertex color baked from
  radial displacement (bumps bright, pits dark, mottled). In-code material
  behavior is unchanged; the shared rock materials now sample vertex colors.
- Story Goal: rocks distinct per size, craggy/asymmetric/pitted per the spec
  purpose ("displacement + vertex color"), tri budgets and exact logical
  radii (2.2/1.3/0.7) held, no core change.
- Owner: /root (Blender MCP 5.2.3 + repo). Dependencies: ST-207 (GLB pipeline
  + assetGlbs.test.js) - met at I207; I208 exporter axis-flip lesson applied.
- Acceptance: GLBs load and swap into the pool, nodes VR_Rock_L/M/S +
  material VR_Rock, tris <= 320/120/80, max vertex radius within 5% of CONFIG
  (exact), COLOR_0 present on every primitive, browser smoke console 0/0,
  distinct silhouettes visually confirmed (Blender viewport + in-game).
- Build notes: Blender 5.2.3 findings this pass: (1) the glTF exporter only
  emits COLOR_0 when the material node tree actually uses a Color Attribute
  node (wire 'Col' -> Base Color; the first export silently dropped the colors
  with a log warning); (2) the export_colors keyword no longer exists;
  (3) ShaderNodeVertexColor.data_type is gone; (4) mathutils Vector has no
  .distance (use (a-b).length) and len(Vector) returns the COMPONENT COUNT (3),
  not magnitude - the first radius pass scaled by radius/3 (caught by the GLB
  audit max-radius check, fixed with .length). Geometry: L = icosphere
  subdiv 3 (320 tris = budget cap, dents only - no inset headroom), M =
  subdiv 2 + 4 inset craters (104 tris), S = subdiv 2 (80 tris exact).
  Displacement = sum of directional sine fields (a product-of-sines field has
  dead zones that left v1 rocks too round - rejected on visual review) +
  white noise (S spikiest: caggy 0.40/0.32/0.30, noise 0.08/0.05/0.02-0.03).
  Vertex color = clamp(1 + 1.4*(|v|-1), 0.45, 1.2) + per-vertex mottle,
  exported as COLOR_0. Pre-rotation (x,y,z)->(x,-z,y) applied per the I208
  axis fix (axis-agnostic for rocks, done for consistency).
- Changed: assets/asteroid_L.glb (37.7 KB, 320 tris), assets/asteroid_M.glb
  (12.2 KB, 104 tris), assets/asteroid_S.glb (10.3 KB, 80 tris),
  src/render/asteroidRenderer.js (mat/matBelt/matGold vertexColors:true;
  bakeVariant bakes a white color attribute for the procedural fallback;
  setGLB defensively adds a white color attribute when a GLB lacks COLOR_0),
  tests/assetGlbs.test.js (meshStats exposes hasColor; asteroid test asserts
  COLOR_0 per primitive), design/asset-spec.md (I209 amendment),
  CHANGELOG.md, production/iterations.md, production/session-state/active.md.
- Verification: npm test 633/633 (COLOR_0 assertions live against the new
  GLBs); node scripts/build.mjs exit 0; GLB structural audit (node, raw
  container parse): nodes/mats/budgets/radii exact 2.2000/1.3000/0.7000;
  browser smoke (fresh context ?nocache=209a, MCP browser closed first per

    toward its centroid -> 240 unique verts, 80 independent gapped plates;
    normalized to max vertex radius EXACT 1.0. No vertex colors (the additive
    shellMats in code are authoritative; armor-level dimming is the read).

GOTCHAS: from_pydata needs explicit face index tuples (first export silently produced 0 primitives); img.pixels is always RGBA (4 floats/px) even with alpha=False; np.mgrid with two slices returns a tuple; 2-D boolean masks must be flattened to index np.nonzero arrays.

KEY FIX: tick(dt, intent) never mirrored the scripted intent into input.state (by design: input.tick is skipped for scripted substeps), so every render-layer read of input.state.thrust - the ST-063 flame AND the new trail - saw (0,0) under the scripted pilot; tick now mirrors intent.thrust.x/y + intent.firing into input.state when an intent is present (no bare advance() callers exist; the real RAF path passes no intent and is untouched).

## I215 - E9 test batch: 18 new core tests for the thinnest coverage (cgs-standards-tests) (ST-215)

- Summary: 18 new pure-logic tests in one new file deepening the thinnest core coverage the I215 audit found (ship 5, enemyBullets 4, mines 4, lives 2, recording 2, rng 1). No src change - 3 first-run failures were test-side corrections against engine-correct behavior (step counts, measured heading order) plus 1 assertion rewritten to pin the actual documented rule (non-unit rim normal).
- Story Goal: ST-215 - E9 test batch (15+ new tests) covering the least-tested core modules.
- Owner + Dependencies: tests/ only; read-only over src/core/ (no engine change warranted).
- Acceptance: >=15 new tests green; 670/670 total; build 0; zero behavior change.
- Build notes: the 4 first-run failures, diagnosed: (1) 300 full-thrust steps (5 s) drive the ship into the rim clamp (vel *= 0.6 stuck), masking the cap - 120 steps (2 s) reaches the 14 cap at ~1.24 s and stays ~22 u in (rim 25.1). (2) The 3 s decay residual 10*exp(-1.6*3) = 0.0823 IS the engine-correct value; 5 s -> 10*exp(-8) = 0.0034. (3) the mine turn test computed `before` from the spawn heading (vel overwritten after the measurement). (4) the rim reflection uses a non-unit normal n = pos/(|pos|+radius) - same rule as asteroids - so the push-back undershoots ~3% at r~25; the sub-cm overshoot self-corrects next step, so the assertion pins inward heading + 0.05 tolerance with a comment.
- Changed: tests/e9Batch.test.js (NEW, 18 tests: ship cap/decay/rim-clamp/damage+invuln/aim-ease; enemyBullets maxConcurrent/exact-4.0s-life/rim-cull-arena+2/clear()-id-reset; mines maxConcurrent/turn-cap-1.6rad-s/rim-bounce-inward/cadence+range+takeFires-drain; lives floor+reset/alive-flip; recording O-PLAYING-only+no-rearm/save-on-game-over-gates; rng same-seed-identity+range/int-inclusive-bounds).
- Verification: 670/670 (was 652, +18); build exit 0; dist unchanged 1939.2 KB raw / 501.3 KB gzip; no src/render change -> no browser smoke required.
- Result: DONE. ST-215 closed. Next I216 ST-216 (G9.0 gate + docs + log audit 1..216).

## I217 - Post-processing polish: half-res bloom + boss phase-2 hit-stop (cgs-dev-story) (ST-217)

**Summary:** Closed ST-217 (art-direction research 2026-11-10, levers 1/2/3/4). (1) Half-res bloom: on every resize the bloom pass's internal bright pass + 5-mip chain is forced to half the composer resolution - the vendor pass downsamples its input 2x again, so the chain runs at 1/4 of the full frame and the pass composites the auto-upscaled glow at full res (visually unchanged). (2) Boss phase-2 (enrage) hit-stop: 40 ms sim-freeze, the only new juice of the spec - hit-stop only, no shake/flash (phase < death in the juice hierarchy). Levers 2 and 3 verified already-done: particle trails are already THREE.AdditiveBlending (particles.js line 23) and the boss-kill flash is already the ST-179 flashTable bossDeath entry (main.js juice branch) - documented, no code.

**Story Goal:** Cut post-processing frame cost with no visible loss; add an impact beat on boss enrage.

**Owner + Dependencies:** main agent; depends on I179 flashTable + I133 bossPhase2 core emit (both reused, unchanged).

**Acceptance:**
1. Bloom internal targets at half composer res on every resize - code + measured (see Verification).
2. CONFIG.fx.hitStop.bossPhase2 inside the 30-50 ms research window, below bomb (0.07) and bossDeath (0.09) - unit-pinned.
3. main.js juice branch on bossPhase2 (hit-stop + tally) inside the motion-gated chain - code-verified; core emit unit-tested for BOTH boss variants through full World steps.
4. 676/676 tests, build 0, browser smoke green (rendering changed).

**Build notes:**
- src/render/bloom.js: resize(w,h) now also sets bloomPass.resolution.set(hw,hh) + bloomPass.setSize(hw,hh) (hw/hh = floor(w*0.5), min 1); ST-217 comment block documents the effective 1/4 (vendor setSize halves again).
- src/core/config.js: fx.hitStop.bossPhase2: 0.04.
- src/main.js: else if (e.type === 'bossPhase2') branch after bossSpawn - hitStop = max(hitStop, 0.04) + juiceTally.record; motion-gated like the rest of the chain.
- tests/bossPhase2.test.js REPAIRED + EXTENDED: the prior ST-217 draft had CLOBBERED the 8 I133 tests in this same file (root cause of the 668-instead-of-676 count anomaly). The 8 I133 tests are restored verbatim; 6 new ST-217 tests appended: config window; enrage emits exactly once with finite pos + exact hpFrac (seed 61); no re-emit across 60 re-hits kept at 40% HP (seed 62); full-HP hits never emit (seed 63); fresh boss re-arms the flag (seed 64, one bullet per step kill + respawn); Splicer variant single emit (seed 65 - needs a warm-up step: forcing entered = 1 snaps the orbit to the hold radius on the next step, so a pre-placed bullet misses by ~12 u).

**Changed:** src/render/bloom.js, src/core/config.js, src/main.js, tests/bossPhase2.test.js, docs.

**Verification:**
- npm test: 676/676 (was 670 = 652 base + ... net +6 after restoring the 8 clobbered I133 tests; no test lost).
- node scripts/build.mjs: exit 0. dist 1940.6 KB raw (was 1939.2; +1.4 KB code-only), gzip re-measured this iteration (see active.md).

The whole post chain (RenderPass + bloom + OutputPass) is ~11% cheaper in steady state; the bloom chain itself runs at 1/4 internal res.

## I221 - ST-219 - Camera/feel (art research lever 6): state-tied subtle camera

**Skill:** cgs-dev-story. **Story:** ST-219 (E10 Post-E9 Polish).

**Goal (YouTube-grounded, I219):** subtle state-tied camera only - the genre comps (Disasteroids 3D / Solaroids) use near-fixed arena views; dynamic camera = state-driven, never free-floating.

**Levers:**
1. 2-5% camera lead toward the aim point (eased, capped) - the LOOK target leads; the camera position stays on the ship-follow (dodge-read rule: the view does not move under the player).
2. Boss-approach zoom-out of a few degrees (eased in and out) - additive to the existing FOV stack (base + speed + kick).
3. Slow menu orbit drift (MENU state only, ~105 s per revolution) - phase 0 = the legacy (0,9,13) view, so boot is visually unchanged.
View FIXED during normal PLAYING (no new motion beyond the look-target lead). Motion-gated: reduced motion freezes the orbit and disables the aim lead; the boss zoom is FOV-only (same class as the pre-existing fovSpeed, not gated). FX-tier: camera feel is deliberately independent of the FX quality setting (negligible cost, not particle FX - documented decision).

**Changed:**
- src/core/config.js (camera block + ST-219 tunables: aimLeadFrac 0.03, aimLeadCap 1.2, bossZoomFov 2.5, bossZoomRange 14, bossZoomDamp 3, menuOrbitRate 0.06)
- src/main.js (follow-camera block: MENU orbit targets (radius 13, phase advances at 0.06 rad/s); PLAYING look-target lead = min(ship->aim * 0.03, 1.2) while the pointer is active; boss zoom = (1 - dist/14 clamped) * 2.5 degrees while a boss is alive, eased at 3/s; targetFov += bossZoomFov; NEW __vr.cameraFeel() probe {menuOrbitA, bossZoomFov})

No test changes (render-layer only - scope-check-E10 notes ST-219 as "the only render-only lever with no test surface beyond browser"); no asset changes.

**Verification:**
- npm test 677/677 (unchanged); node scripts/build.mjs exit 0.
- Browser smoke ?nocache=221a (browser closed first): 16/16 assets + skybox ready.
- Menu orbit: menuOrbitA 0.28 -> 1.03 rad at 0.06 rad/s (gap-consistent; ~105 s/rev); camera azimuth 0.27 -> 1.02 rad (damp-lags the target - smooth); A/B screenshots .evidence/I221/01-menu-orbit-a.png + 02-menu-orbit-b.png (visibly rotated vantage: arena ring + starfield + ship).
- Aim lead (ship pinned at origin, forward from aim angle): base 0/0; pointer aim ndc (0.9,-0.8) -> lead +0.223/+0.334 (3% of the ship->aim offset, under cap); release -> 0/0 (eased out); touchDrag aim 50 units ahead -> lead EXACTLY +1.2 x (0.03*50 = 1.5 capped to 1.2); after clear -> 0.

## I230 - ST-226 - skybox art pass: Blender 5.2 node-baked equirect v5 + 1024x512 JPEG swap (cgs-dev-story + Blender MCP)
- Story: ST-226 (E12) - replace the I212 flat-gradient PNG with a Blender-baked layered nebula equirect; budget-staged, net delta <= 40 KB raw; PMREM IBL hook unchanged.
- Root cause of the v1..v3 zero-gas failure FOUND (constant probes, 512x256 EMIT bakes): Blender 5.2 VectorMath LENGTH and NORMALIZE are broken - LENGTH((3,4,0)) and LENGTH((0.5,0,0)) both bake to (1,1,1); NORMALIZE((3,4,0)) bakes to the component-wise MEAN (0.467,0.467,0.467) instead of (0.6,0.8,0) - while DOT_PRODUCT works exactly (1.0/0.0/1.0 on constant probes; Generated.x exact on a varying probe, p50 0.503). The v3 graph fed a degenerate DIR (broken normalize) into the gas chain, which is why every isolated sub-piece worked but the full sum zeroed.
- Fix = math-only unit direction, ZERO VectorMath nodes: dir = SeparateXYZ(Generated) - 0.5 per channel; |dir|^2 via MULTIPLY/MULTIPLY_ADD; inv = INVERSE_SQRT; U = dir*inv; band dot via MULTIPLY/MULTIPLY_ADD chain; band = EXPONENT(-k d^2) (Math EXPONENT = 2^x). Two more 5.2 gotchas: the Noise node has NO vector offset input (the Offset socket is a float) - gas/dust/hero offsets applied as per-channel ADDs on U; and the bake target node select must be committed with tree.update_tag() or the bake silently no-ops with "No active and selected image texture node found".
- Art iterations: v4 (full graph first alive) = gas p50 0.40 linear + clipped band core = 3x too bright; v5 FINAL = gas x0.15, bandGlow x0.3, band k 150->100 + boost 0.75->0.5, cyan retuned (0.18,0.75,0.95)->(0.25,0.68,0.92) to kill the 8-bit green cast on faint wisps (R<<G quantizes greenish). Final bake stats (2048x1024, linear): R p50 0.0666 / G 0.0529 / B 0.0685, p99 0.23/0.19/0.25, max 1.0 (band core + hero cores), 0 NaN - dark navy with layered cyan/magenta/gold wisps, dust lanes, faint off-axis band, dense clustered starfield + compact hero stars; sky subordinate per spec.
- Encode pick: 1024x512 PNG = 679.6 KB (the dense 1px starfield kills PNG compression; the v3 near-black sky was 7 KB) -> 1024x512 JPEG = 73.2 KB (74,915 B; net +32.0 KB raw vs the 41.2 KB PNG, within the <= 40 KB budget; same 1024x512 resolution as the I212 asset). assets/skybox_equirect.jpg installed, skybox_equirect.png removed; src/main.js URL updated; tests/skyboxTexture.test.js rewritten as a JPEG audit (SOI magic via readUInt32BE(0)>>>8, SOF0 1024x512 walk, <= 120 KB). skybox.js unchanged (TextureLoader is format-agnostic); PMREM IBL unchanged.
- Changed: assets/skybox_equirect.jpg (new), assets/skybox_equirect.png (removed), src/main.js (URL only), tests/skyboxTexture.test.js (PNG->JPEG audit), docs (iterations/CHANGELOG/active).
- Verification: 697/697 (1 test rewritten in place; SOI mask fixed from & 0xffffff to >>> 8 after the first red run), build 0, dist 1994.5 raw / 543.2 gzip KB (<=2048/<=550, 105 files; gzip headroom now 6.8 KB = WATCH - JPEG does not re-compress under gzip).
- Browser smoke 230a (native dpr 1.25, 60 Hz): skybox.ready === true on the new URL, console 0/0 (incl. after FX cycling + gameplay); MENU A/B vs the I227 baseline = layered nebula + starfield on dark navy with all UI/grid/ship popping (subordinate sky); in-flight at FX high/low/off: rocks + ship + arena grid readable at every tier (sky is tier-independent, correct) + IBL tints visible on rock faces; frame probe 787 f (30 warm-up excluded): avg 16.67 / p95 18.9 / max 42.4 ms (single spike at a screenshot; only 1 frame > 30 ms; 259 frames in the 17.5-18.9 ms band = next-vsync quantum jitter, same character as the I225/I229 probes; no sustained degradation, no skybox upload hitch - texture loaded pre-gameplay).
- Accepted art note: magenta x gold gas-overlap zones read as a warm muddy brown in some camera directions (palette overlap physics; readability intact at all FX tiers) - revisit only on playtest request (do-not-over-engineer).
- Demo pin: structurally untouched (render/asset layer only; unit-covered in 697); browser re-verify scheduled at ST-227 (I231).
- Result: DONE
- Follow-up: I231 = ST-227 (E12 full regression + G12.0 gate + closeout; cgs-regression-suite + cgs-gate-check + cgs-milestone-review)
## I231 - ST-227 - E12 regression round + G12.0 gate PASS + E12 CLOSED (cgs-regression-suite + cgs-gate-check + cgs-milestone-review)
- Story: ST-227 (E12) - E12 full regression round, G12.0 closeout gate, milestone docs. No src changes except the 1-line F-marker gate fix below.
- GATE FIX (src/main.js, 1 line moved): the F hotkey branch ran applyFxTier(next, 'hotkey') BEFORE adaptive.userSet(next); updateFxMarker() (inside applyFxTier) reads adaptive.state().autoLow, so after an auto-downgrade the first user F left a stale 'AUTO: LOW' chip on both panels even though the user had just taken ownership of the tier (the R-E12-4 surface). Fix = userSet now runs before applyFxTier in the F branch (the panel path was already in this order). userSet is safe to run first: it only mutates the pure ladder (tier/autoLow/userLocked/belowStreak) + fires the ignored {type:'user'} callback; it never touches storage/fxCount/bloom/DPR.
- Pre-fix live repro (231b, dpr-2 tab): boot DPR 2 -> forced auto-downgrade (reset + 165 x noteFrame(50)) -> both chips opacity 1 + toast 'FX AUTO-SET TO LOW (LOW FPS)' + storage untouched; first F (stored high -> low) -> chips STILL opacity 1 (stale marker bug confirmed).
- Post-fix verify (231c, fresh dpr-2 tab, ?nocache=231c): boot DPR 2 / MENU / skybox ready on the new JPEG / both chips opacity 0; Enter -> PLAYING; forced auto-downgrade -> tier low / autoLow true / chips 1+1 / 'DPR 2.00 -> 1.50 (FX low, auto-downgrade)' + adaptive line + toast + storage still 'high' (untouched); FIRST user F -> chips 0+0 (marker cleared), autoLow false, userLocked true, stored 'low', NO DPR line (1.5 target = current, correct no-op), toast 'FX LOW'; further F cycles stay cleared (off) then restore (high -> 'DPR 1.50 -> 2.00 (FX high, hotkey)' + bloom on, chips stay 0); second auto-downgrade cycle: chip visibly 'AUTO: LOW' beside FX QUALITY on the pause panel while the select still shows the stored HIGH (.evidence/I231/03-pause-autolow-marker-in.png); idle death -> GAME_OVER -> Enter live restart intact; ship/asteroid positions all finite (no NaN); console 0 errors / 0 warnings - all 7 lines accounted for (the lone extra 'adaptive quality' line with no DPR line = a reset()-re-armed downgrade whose 1.5 target already equaled the current pixelRatio: applyDprForTier no-op by design).

## I245 - ST-238 explosion layering pass (cgs-dev-story)
- Story: E15 ST-238 - the Brackeys-grounded VFX layering recipe (sparks + shockwave + debris) for Void Runner: ONE pooled additive shockwave ring on class-scaled kills. NO flash/hit-stop on plain kills (I224 plain-kill juice budget holds); rings are motion-gated; FX high 1 ring / low 1 ring / off 0 (zero-leak).
- Changed: NEW src/core/shockwave.js (pure, DOM/three-free: shockwaveRadius(classKey, cfg) table lookup, 0 for unmapped/invalid - shakeTable pattern) + NEW src/render/shockwave.js (createShockwaves: ONE shared RingGeometry(0.82,1.0,40) = 80 tris rotateX(-PI/2) flat in XZ; pool of 8 meshes with per-entry MeshBasicMaterial (additive blending, depthWrite false, DoubleSide, frustumCulled false); ring-buffer cursor per particles.js precedent; parked = visible false + opacity 0; animate scale radius*(0.3+0.7p), opacity 0.85*(1-p) over life 0.42 s; sim (x,y) -> mesh (x, 0.35, y)) + NEW tests/shockwave.test.js (9 tests: config block sanity, all 6 spec rungs present, S<M<L, drone<stinger<warden, band 1..5 u, mapped returns table, unmapped mine/flyer/boss/splicer -> 0, invalid args -> 0, non-finite/negative/zero/string rung -> 0) + juiceTally JUICE_CHANNELS 5 -> 6 (+ring, schema pin test updated + 1 new test) + CONFIG.fx.shockwave { life 0.42, baseOpacity 0.85, pool 8, height 0.35, classes { S 1.6, M 2.4, L 3.4, drone 1.6, stinger 2.0, warden 3.0 } } + main.js (imports, createShockwaves after particles, kill-branch spawn from the unified kill `size`, shockwaves.sync(dt) after particles.sync(dt), __vr.shockwaveLive()/__vr.shockwaveRings() probes).
- Design: class keys = the `size` values carried by the unified kill event (game.js emits kill for rocks L/M/S, mine, boss, warden, drone, stinger, flyer). Table covers EXACTLY the spec classes; mine/flyer/boss deliberately get NO ring (spec-limited; boss/wardenDeath already own the big-juice events; table-driven so a future rung is a one-line config change). Colors: golden fff0a0, warden b18cff (boss purple family), drone 6fe8ff, stinger d8f4ff, rocks ffb24a. Zero per-kill allocation (R-E15-3): shared geometry, pooled meshes, module-scope scratch _killCh/SW_COLORS. Ring recorded in juiceTally as channel `ring` on the kill row (punch unchanged).
- Verification: npm test 742/742 (732 + 9 shockwave + 1 juiceTally) fresh this session, build 0, dist 113 files / 2026.5 raw / 538.2 gzip KB (headroom 21.5/11.8 vs 2048/550 caps; +2.7 gz vs the I244 535.5 baseline). Browser smoke ?nocache=i245: baseline pool 8 / 1 shared 80-tri geo / 8 mats / additive / all parked; kills AFTER the adaptive tier was established: L radius 3.4, S radius 1.6 (L/S scale ratio exactly 3.4/1.6 = class scaling), drone 1.6/6fe8ff, stinger 2.0/d8f4ff, golden L fff0a0, warden 3.0/b18cff, unmapped (flyer, mine) -> live 0; all rings expire to parked (visible false, opacity 0); juiceTally kill row punch 9 / ring 6 (rings = exactly the 6 mapped kills); wardenDeath row ring 0; FX-off via REAL F keydowns (3 presses high -> low -> off): kill L + flyerDeath -> ring delta 0 / particle delta 0, all parked, ring total unchanged - zero-leak EXACT; reduced motion via applySettings (full settings object): all juice deltas 0 (ring/particles/punch/flash); 240-tick NaN sweep 0 violations (ship pos/vel, rocks, all 8 rings scale/pos/opacity, particle attributes); console 0 errors / 0 warnings (7 designed adaptive INFOs - VM tab throttled 4-6 fps).
- Boot-timing finding (documented, not a defect): fxCount is 0 until the first adaptive window sets the tier (~2 s after boot), so kills in that window spawn NO burst AND no ring - consistent with pre-existing particle behavior (the ring is gated on fxCount > 0 by construction).
- Probe artifact (not a bug): kill feed showed "L ROCK +undefined" because hand-emitted kill events omit `points` (real kills carry it).
- Screenshots .evidence/I245/: 01-rings-midflight-high (wide in-flight; ring subtle against nebula - acceptable flight shot), 02-ring-closeup-high (frozen-frame close-up: ring at ~70% expansion, camera (0,5,9) - amber ring clearly visible), 03-menu-fresh-boot (post-reload menu after settings restore: stored fx high = DEFAULT_SETTINGS, adaptive userLocked false).
- Deterministic screenshot technique (new, proven): the loop renders EVERY rAF frame regardless of state and syncVisuals re-derives the camera from camPos each frame, so to freeze: save window.__vr.__origRAF, set window.requestAnimationFrame = () => 0 (the loop reschedules via the global each frame -> halts), emit + v.advance(1/60, INTENT) (events drain ONLY in advance), pin the ring age/scale/opacity, set the camera, v.bloom.render(), screenshot, then restore rAF in a follow-up evaluate.

## I254 - ST-245 arena ring v2 + world presentation: Blender two-node GLB (band + 12 tick chevrons) (cgs-dev-story + Blender MCP)
- Story: ST-245 (E17). The arena ring (I212) - a single 48-tri flat band - is the world boundary anchor and the genre vector-grid signature (I252 YouTube grounding: Geometry Wars / Neon Wars neon-vector boundary language), underplayed. v2 adds 12 inner radial tick chevrons at the major seams as a brighter accent crossing the 0.72 bloom threshold (the intended neon accent; band luma stays below it), in one two-node GLB.
- Blender (5.2.3 LTS, MCP addon 1.8): two nodes in one scene - VR_ArenaRing outer band (24 plates 11 deg / 4 deg gaps, inner 0.94 / outer 1.0; 48 verts / 48 tris) + VR_ArenaRing_Ticks (12 triangle wedges at the major seams every 30 deg, base r 0.895 half-width 0.016 rad -> tip r 0.93; 36 verts / 12 tris). Total 60 tris <= 120 budget. Flat XZ (maxAbsY 0), 84/84 +Y normals, indexed (144 band + 36 ticks uint16 indices), POSITION+NORMAL only (no COLOR_0 - materials stay in-code). 4032 B <= 4096 B (v1: 4784 B / 48 tris single node -> v2 -752 B for +12 tris).
- Blender gotchas (5.2.3 MCP): (1) a leftover I230 scene (SkyboxI230) in bpy.data.scenes made the exporter write the nodes to the NON-DEFAULT scene - GLTFLoader (scene index 0) would have loaded an EMPTY ring; purged all scenes except one, named it Scene, set bpy.context.window.scene (bpy.context.scene is READ-ONLY in MCP exec). (2) Mesh.update() takes only (calc_edges, calc_edges_loose) - no calculate_normals; call me.update() then read p.normal. (3) flat-shaded from_pydata exports DE-INDEXED (first attempt: 96v/48t band, 5688 B) - use_smooth=True on all faces before export (all coplanar +Y so smooth normals stay (0,1,0) and the indexed write holds). (4) JSON bloat: Metallic=Roughness=1.0 (glTF defaults -> keys omitted), use_backface_culling=True (drops doubleSided), direct short-decimal base colors.
- Assets: assets/arena_ring.glb v2 - 2 nodes / 2 materials, exactly 60 tris, 4032 B (band maxR ~1.0 / inner ~0.94; ticks maxR < 0.94 / minR >= 0.88; per-primitive flat / +Y / radius in the pin).
- Render: src/render/arenaRing.js rewritten to a two-mesh THREE.Group (name ArenaRing, scale.setScalar(26)) - band mesh UNCHANGED (0x4a6cff op 0.55, DoubleSide, depthWrite false, y 0.03); ticks = MeshBasic 0xdfe9ff op 0.9 at y 0.035 (5 mm above the band, no z-fight), luma ~0.91 CROSSES the 0.72 bloom threshold = the intended neon accent (band luma ~0.45 stays below). Node-name matching (VR_ArenaRing_Ticks first, exact ===) with v1 FALLBACK (no ticks node -> old single-mesh behavior). API { useGLB, mesh(), tickMesh(), group(), geo() } - main.js line 155 (arenaRing.useGLB(entry.object)) UNCHANGED; __vr.arenaRing auto-exposes the new methods.
- Tests: tests/assetGlbs.test.js arena pin rewritten - 2 nodes + 2 materials, no COLOR_0, EXACTLY 60 tris (band 48 + ticks 12), <= 4096 B, per-primitive flat / +Y / radius via a new primStats() (band maxR ~1.0 / inner ~0.94; ticks maxR < 0.94 / minR >= 0.88); meshStats prims now expose idxAcc and the face loop reads the index accessor (5123/5125) - the old loop assumed UNINDEXED (posAcc.count = index count) and broke on the indexed v2 (symptom: 0 !== 16). BUDGETS.arenaRing unchanged (120).
- Design: design/asset-spec.md arena entry rewritten to v2 (v1 provenance kept inline).
- Browser (?nocache=i254b, dev :5173, fresh boot): MENU, 18/18 assets ready; arena group x26; band 48 tris y 0.03 4a6cff + ticks 12 tris y 0.035 dfe9ff; hidden->shown delta = +1 scene call / +12 tris (bloom ON: +2 / +24 - the bloom pass mirrors, expected); NaN scan (arena pos / normals, camera, ship) all 0. BLOOM A/B (forced on/off, same-frame readPixels at the projected tick + crops): tick core luma 210 in BOTH states (RGB ~ (209,210,216) = 0xdfe9ff cool white), background ~74-86; bloom ON widens the bright core to a 13 px plateau (halo) vs OFF 3 px core + AA tail -> halo present exactly where intended, band below threshold in both. ## I255 - ST-246 E17 regression round + G17.0 gate + closeout (cgs-regression-suite + cgs-gate-check + cgs-milestone-review) - regression-round finding: adaptive FX desync found + fixed
- Story: ST-246 (E17). The final E17 story: a full regression sweep across the E17 delta (asteroid v3 GLB + arena ring v2), the G17.0 gate, and closeout. NOT docs-only: the FX-off zero-leak probe surfaced a pre-existing latent defect (Finding below) that was fixed + pinned with 6 new tests.
- Finding + fix (pre-existing, surfaced by the regression sweep - not an E17 regression): createAdaptiveQuality booted its ladder at a phantom 'high' (userLocked:false) because adaptive.userSet was only ever called from the settings panels + F-hotkey, never at boot - so a persisted 'off' FX tier was silently overridden by the first low-FPS auto-downgrade (high->low, fxCount 0 -> 16), contradicting ST-225 intent (a stored choice is never overwritten). Confirmed live pre-fix: stored 'off' -> state() {tier:'low', autoLow:true, userLocked:false}. Fix: createAdaptiveQuality(cfg, onDowngrade, initialTier='high') now reconciles the ladder at boot - bootTier = 'low'/'off' when initialTier is one of those, else 'high'; userLocked = bootTier !== 'high' (a stored non-high tier is a locked user prior); reset() restores the boot tier. main.js passes liveSettings.fx as the 3rd arg (2-arg callers unchanged -> default 'high' -> all pre-existing tests pass unmodified).
- Fix verification (browser ?nocache=i255fix2/final2, dev :5173): stored 'off' now boots {tier:'off', userLocked:true} and stays 'off' through 20 starved noteFrame(0.5) frames (pre-fix: drifted to 'low'). FX-off zero-leak now strict: a real kill (bullet fired at a rock center, killDelta 1) -> particleLive 0/0 + shockwaveLive 0/0 (the pre-fix probe was confounded by the drift: 48/3); ST-177 pickup readability floor = EXACTLY 8 at FX off.
- Regression sweep (isolated browser tab; the user's live tab untouched): pool caps - rock pool 29 / bullets 64 / enemy bullets 24 / mines 10 / drones 6 / stingers 4 / flyers 4 / boss 3-mesh / warden 3-mesh / particles 512 / shockwave rings 8. Determinism: fresh 1080-tick seed-393 scripted-intent run 0 NaN (per-tick ship finiteness + full 1536-float particle scans every 120 ticks + final scan). Live state machine: all 11 legal transitions via real keys/mouse (P/Q/Enter + '3' replay speed; REPLAYS -> DEMO PILOT real mouse click; Q from replay GAME_OVER exits to menu). Demo pin EXACT via real UI: stops at idx 2169/2220 (x4) with score 11334 / kills 57 / wave 2 / maxCombo 15 / shots 57-of-198 (29%) / 0:36 - byte-identical to the I254 pin; the watched demo never writes scoreboards (best 36426 unchanged, scores list head 36426). Frame probe (MENU, FX off): 15 calls / 208 tris = I253 baseline + the I254 ring delta (+1 call / +12 tris) - delta from E17 open ~0. Console 0/0 (fresh page).
- Gate G17.0 (epics.md): PASS - asteroid GLB 18/18 + extent parity + class A/B (L/M/S side-by-side) + golden/shielded/belt live checks + NET NEGATIVE raw size vs I251 (I253); ring v2 two-node GLB + two-mesh render + +1 bounded draw call + frame-probe delta ~0 + bloom band A/B (I254); demo pin EXACT via real UI (2169/2220, 11334, 57, 36.15 s, wave 2) (this iteration); fresh tests + build + dist in budget (771/771, build 0, headroom 57.5 raw / 16.5 gz >= 8) (this iteration); regression green (this iteration: 0 NaN + caps + transitions + strict zero-leak); log audit sequential 1..255 (below).
- Verification: 771/771 (765 + 6), build 0, dist 113 files 1991.1 raw / 533.5 gz6 KB (headroom 57.5 raw / 16.5 gz - floor >= 8 HOLDS; the small code additions sit well inside budget).
- Changed: src/core/adaptiveQuality.js (boot-tier reconciliation), src/main.js (passes liveSettings.fx), tests/adaptiveQuality.test.js (+6 I255 tests: off-stays-off / low-stays-low / default still downgrades / explicit high downgrades / invalid -> high / reset() restores the stored tier), CHANGELOG.md (+I255), production/iterations.md (+I255), production/session-state/active.md (header + story/iteration/tests + I255 note), production/stories.md (ST-246 DONE + E17 CLOSED), production/epics.md (E17 Result CLOSED + R-E17-2/-3 resolved + R-E17-5), production/timeline.md (+E17 closed). blender/ + .evidence/ stay UNTRACKED.

## I257 - ST-247 rock material + lighting hero pass (cgs-dev-story + Blender MCP) - warm rock: the cool rim was the sole root cause (the readPixels stale buffer masked it)
- Story: ST-247 (E18). Rock palette + lighting: Blender COLOR_0 rebake + scene light rebalance + the bloom-band audit (acceptance: table vs the 183.6 luma threshold, FX high + off A/B; golden/belt re-verify).
- Tooling bug (the trap that cost the arc): renderer.render() + gl.readPixels with preserveDrawingBuffer:false returns a STALE composited buffer - readPixels returned IDENTICAL pixel values across radically different scene states (lights off, material painted red, rim 0). readPixels is unusable on this page. Only trustworthy channels: page.screenshot() + PNG parsing in Node (device 1920x913 = 1.25x client 1536x730) + visual inspection of the PNG.
- Root cause (decisive screenshot/PNG A/B ladder, .evidence/I257/19..23): the periwinkle WAS the cool rim onBeforeCompile term - the ENTIRE cool cast. Single M rock at arena center, other pools hidden: A key(6,12,4)+rim 0x8fa8d8@0.22 = R111.8/G111.3/B133.1 (blue); B rim OFF = 67.2/45.9/30.7 (warm brown, R=2.2xB); C camkey(3,9,10)+rim = 119.7/116.3/135.7 (still blue - the rim masks the key); D camkey + rim off = 79.0/54.6/36.6 (warm, brighter); E black control = 1/0/0 (pipeline valid); envMapIntensity 0.18 vs 0.0 = dRGB <= 1.5 (negligible - rough dielectric + dark nebula PMREM; the knob is real but weak). Mechanism: low-poly facets sit at grazing angles, so pow(1-dot(N,V),5) stays ~1 over most of a low-poly rock visible surface, and ACES amplifies the additive rim.
- Chosen (D3): warm rim 0xd8a878 @ 0.12 (exponent 5 unchanged) + camera-side warm key dir.position (6,12,4) -> (3,9,10) (camera-facing facets get N.L ~0.7 instead of ~0.28) + config env.rock 0.18 -> 0.0 (rocks fully decoupled from the blue-violet nebula PMREM; ship/belt keep the 0.35 fill). D3 sample: 127.4/92.9/62.0 (R=2.05xB, luma ~96) - warm tan, comfortably under the 183.6 bloom threshold.
- Blender COLOR_0 rebake (asteroid L/M/S): warm sRGB bake (per-vertex R>=G>=B, band [0.44,1.21]); geometry / radii / node names / budgets UNCHANGED (L 8912 B/162v, M 3724 B/54v, S 3148 B/42v). tests/assetGlbs.test.js warm-band pin (glTF accessor type field for the COLOR_0 stride, NOT itemSize).
- Code: src/render/asteroidRenderer.js (rim uniforms + comment block rewritten with the A/B numbers; setEnvIntensity API unchanged), src/main.js (key position + light-set comment), src/core/config.js (env: {intensity:0.35, rock:0.0}).

## I258 - ST-248 bullet/tracer + explosion palette harmony (cgs-dev-story) - player tracers = cockpit-cyan accent + overdrive heat; rock explosion = warm ember ramp

- Story: ST-248 (E18 story 2 of 4; I258 of I257..I260).
- Design decisions:
  - Player bullet L1 0xbff4ff -> 0x7fe4ff: cockpit-cyan accent family (cockpitMat emissive 0x59d8ff @1.4) so "yours" reads as a family at kill rate; L2/L3 unchanged (0x8affc0 / 0xffd27a).
  - Overdrive heat shift: bulletLevelOverdrive [0xd2f5ff, 0xd6ffec, 0xfff1e1] - white-hot, hue-preserving per level, all three over the 183.6 bloom threshold so tracers glow while overdrive is live at the high tier. Magenta overdrive pickup identity unchanged.
  - Rock-kill explosion = warm ember ramp [0xfff0c8 core (240.3), 0xffa44a mid (176.8), 0xd96a2a tail (125.0)] - R>=G>=B every stop, harmonized to the I257 warm rocks (periwinkle era over).
  - killRing explicit 0xffa44a: the plain-kill shockwave ring stays amber (previously inherited the rock-palette default).
  - Enemy bolt values UNCHANGED (0xff4a2a) but moved to CONFIG.fx.enemyBullet (data-driven); player/enemy luma separation 98.3 preserved.
  - ST-177 FX-off floor: FLOOR=50 vs measured bg max nebula 51 (I257); grid 41.9 (CONFIG.arena.gridBright/gridDim now config-owned). Player 157.5, overdrive 187.3, enemy 59.2 (margin 9 - enemy intentionally dimmer).
